Control-path qualification and verified ECP5 mapping - 2026-10-31

Requirements: WSE-STD-BRAM-RAM sections 5.3/8/11 and the existing owner,
stream and window implementation profiles. The 100MHz clock, 40,000-clock
shared DMA window, 133,333-clock audio block, N=64, K=2/4/8, int16 storage,
phase precision, headroom, RNE and saturation remain unchanged.

Owner next-state bits now directly OR the same destination predicates instead
of nesting state muxes. All seven values and three stored bits are unchanged.
tools/check_hdl_control.py proves every encoded state and every binary event/
status input against an independent transition-policy case table, including
invalid-state recovery. No reset/reachability/unique-tag assumption is needed.

The registered DMA payload fields are sampled every clock; their existing
registered enable is the consumer qualifier. The BRAM payload read similarly
samples every clock, with existing read_pending qualifying the FIFO write.
Local all-input proofs establish equal data whenever consumed, for arbitrary
memory/input values and independent prior field contents. Addresses, widths,
FIFO reservations, write ordering and read/payload latency are unchanged.
Inactive field values may differ. Extra invalid-cycle internal activity is
the power tradeoff; board power has not been measured. All actual payload,
publication, stall, deadline, reset and independent audio checks are rerun.

Default mapping still missed 100MHz. LUT4-only mapping and explicit keep
boundaries did not solve it; those probes are retained. The first ABC9 probe
routed at 103.22048MHz but its MFS pass aborted internally. Yosys returned zero
and used the pre-MFS output, so this was not accepted as a clean build.

The selected mapping uses synth_ecp5 -abc9, abc9.nomfs=true and
abc9.verify=true. The clean verified K4 probe produces the identical netlist.
Every final owner/stream/window mapping must have a clean log and one explicit
ABC Networks-are-equivalent result per &verify invocation. Guards reject
missing/failed verification, the observed subtool crash even alongside a
success marker, the disabled MFS pass, altered logs and stale mapping records.
Artifact names include abc9 and inventories include the complete mapping
profile and hashed synthesis log. The netlist's FF, block RAM and distributed
RAM counts remain unchanged; actual LUT/carry/mux costs are recorded below.

ABC's check verifies the extracted mapping network. It is not a complete
RTL-to-device proof, a RAM primitive model, routed-netlist equivalence or board
timing. The installed simulation library declares DP16KD as a black box.
An alternate staged equiv_opt attempt could not run: initial JSON export met
unlowered library processes, and the corrected RTLIL flow hit abc9_ops no_loops.
Neither attempt is counted as proof. Their logs remain available.

The 26 final RTL simulations and 57 formal/evidence tests cover 83 tests on
the modified paths. The 25 unchanged burst/priority/bounds tests retain their
source-matched scope: 108 total distinct tests through scoped runs, not a new
single full-root run. Direct formal tests include observable-error mutants.
No host audio arithmetic, resource recipe or acceptance threshold changed.

Routing uses ECP5-25K speed6 CSFBGA285, out-of-context, 100MHz and seeds1/2/3.
Placement logs now stream to disk and the host execution allowance is 600
seconds. An interrupted 180-second run was resumed with the same seed and
netlist after confirming its process had stopped; the timeout is retained.
This host allowance changes no hardware clock or audio/DMA deadline. The 43
owner/stream/window inventory guards passed again after the logging change
(2.38 seconds); they are already included in the 57-test count above.
No failed seed or result is discarded. Device-wide packing, I/O/global clocks,
adapter/PHY, workload admission and full voice generation/operators remain
open. Mode A storage and the K2 tier cost are still unresolved.

Reproduce: pixi run check-hdl-control; the existing synth-hdl/route-hdl tasks
for coeff-buffers, ccs-stream and dma-window; and the conformance tests.

Lines 224-240 implement nomfs and verify; a downloaded copy is checkpointed.

The ORs\n        // below directly form the same three stored bits, without a priority\n        // mux across state branches. No extra state, latency or encoding.\n        wire next_wait=(current_state==FREE && bank_claim) ||\n                       (current_state==WAITING && !bank_submit && !expire);\n        wire next_fill=(current_state==WAITING && bank_submit && !expire) ||\n                       (current_state==FILLING && !finished && !expire);\n        wire next_ready=(current_state==FILLING && finished && !expire && dma_done_status==0) ||\n                        (current_state==READY && !expire && !bank_acquire);\n        wire next_read=(current_state==READY && !expire && bank_acquire) ||\n                       (current_state==READING && !bank_release);\n        wire next_discard_wait=(current_state==WAITING && !bank_submit && expire) ||\n                               (current_state==DISCARD_WAIT && !bank_submit);\n        wire next_discard_fill=(current_state==WAITING && bank_submit && expire) ||\n                               (current_state==DISCARD_WAIT && bank_submit) ||\n                               (current_state==FILLING && !finished && expire) ||\n                               (current_state==DISCARD_FILL && !finished);\n        wire [2:0] next_state={next_read || next_discard_wait || next_discard_fill,\n                               next_fill || next_ready || next_discard_fill,\n                               next_wait || next_ready || next_discard_wait};\n\n// Independent transition policy: FREE, WAITING, FILLING, READY, READING,\n// DISCARD_WAIT, DISCARD_FILL = 0..6. Unused encoding 7 returns to FREE.\nalways @* begin\n    reference=current_state;\n    case (current_state)\n        0: if (claim && selected==bank) reference=1;\n        1: if (submit && pending_bank==bank) reference=expire ? 6 : 2;\n           else if (expire) reference=5;\n        2: if (finished) reference=(!expire && dma_done_status==0) ? 3 : 0;\n           else if (expire) reference=6;\n        3: if (expire) reference=0;\n           else if (acquire && match_ready) reference=4;\n        4: if (release_valid && release_bank==bank) reference=0;\n        5: if (submit && pending_bank==bank) reference=6;\n        6: if (finished) reference=0;\n        default: reference=0;\n    endcase\nend\nassign same=reference==next_state;\nendmodule\n",
